*GPT message: We have successfully returned to the main deck screen after the failed login attempt. The bug report provides two ways to initiate a sync: tapping the button or a swipe gesture. Since tapping the button requires a login we cannot provide, we must use the alternative method.

The bug report mentions "swipe-up". This can be ambiguous. It could mean a literal swipe from bottom-to-top, or it could be a colloquialism for the "pull-to-sync" gesture, which is a swipe from top-to-bottom. The "pull-to-sync/refresh" gesture is a very common UI pattern in list-based apps. Let's try the standard "pull-to-sync" gesture first, which is a swipe down.

Suggestion:
```json
[
  {
    "action": "swipe",
    "to_direction": "down"
  }
]
```

*GPT message: We are back on the main deck list screen. The bug report mentions two ways to sync: tapping the button (which required a login we don't have) or "swipe-up". The term "swipe-up" is unconventional for a sync action; a "swipe-down" (pull-to-refresh/sync) is far more common on a list screen like this. It's likely the bug report has a typo. Let's try the standard pull-to-sync gesture first.

After triggering the sync, we need to check for the notification.

Suggestion:

    "action": "swipe",
    "to_direction": "down"
  }
]

*GPT message: We are back on the main screen. Our previous attempt to trigger the sync via the "Check media" option was unsuccessful.

The bug report explicitly states two ways to trigger the sync: "swipe-up, tap the button".

1.  **Tap the button:** We can see a "Sync" button in the toolbar. However, it has a warning icon, and our previous attempts to interact with sync-related features led to a login prompt, which we cannot complete.
2.  **Swipe-up:** This is the other method mentioned. In many apps, a "pull-to-refresh" gesture (swiping down from the top of a list) is used to sync. The user might have meant "swipe-down" or there might be a "swipe-up" gesture. Let's first try the most common pattern for syncing a list: a swipe-down (pull-to-refresh) gesture.

We will perform a swipe-down gesture on the main screen to see if it initiates the sync process.
